All checks run from `/Users/ren/repos/bonfire`. Working tree confirmed untouched throughout (verified before/after: 42 files changed in porcelain status, identical `git diff --stat` — `42 files changed, 194 insertions(+), 122 deletions(-)`). No files were modified, committed, or stashed.

| 9 | git status | **CLEAN/UNCHANGED** | Identical before and after this run |

### Notes

**A — Methodology on `pnpm test`:** I ran several `check:*` scripts concurrently with the backgrounded `pnpm test` to save time; this caused CPU contention that tipped 13 borderline-slow tests (across `rpc.test.ts`, `channels-posts.test.ts`, `heron.test.ts`, `incubator/kit-index-race.fix.test.ts`, `wishing-pool.test.ts`, and parts of `communities-rpc.test.ts`/`door.test.ts`) over the default 5000ms timeout. I re-ran those 8 files in isolation afterward (`vitest run` alone, nothing else on the machine) — all passed except the tests already known-genuine, confirming the 13 were false positives, not product bugs.

**B — Two genuine, reproducible bugs, both in the in-flight kind-revision-binding arc:**
- **StateInvalid "Kind installation must own the root revision-binding transaction"** — thrown by `assertRootKindInstallStorage` at `/Users/ren/repos/bonfire/packages/core/src/engine/deployment/kind-deployment.ts:788` whenever kind install runs inside an existing tx. Breaks `packages/std/src/platform/app-deployment.test.ts` (4 tests), `packages/server/src/communities-rpc.test.ts` (deployApp-over-the-wire), `packages/sdk/src/world/deploy.test.ts` (F7 founder deploy), and on Postgres, `packages/server/src/door-install.integration.test.ts`.
- **TypeError "Legacy Kind lineage ... is not one exact sealed snapshot"** — thrown by `restoreExactSealedLegacyLineage` at `/Users/ren/repos/bonfire/packages/core/src/kinds/service/revision-binding/sealed-legacy-proof-source.ts:99`. Breaks `packages/std/src/platform/door.test.ts` setInstallPolicy loosen/re-tighten/stamp-backed (3 tests) and `communities-rpc.test.ts` setInstallPolicy-over-the-wire (surfaces as `HandlerThrew`).
- Postgres-only bonus: `TypeError: Cannot read private member #kindRevisionBindingStoreV2 from an object whose class did not declare it` at `/Users/ren/repos/bonfire/packages/server/src/pg-storage/storage.ts:109` — the getter is invoked with `this` bound to a Proxy (test-injected failure wrapper) rather than the real class instance, so the private-field brand check fails. Breaks `write-path-custody-atomicity.integration.test.ts`'s rollback test.

**C — 6 Postgres-only failures of unclear relation to this arc** (not touching any modified file): `g7-four-surfaces.integration.test.ts` — 3× `PermissionDenied: generated relation ... may only be created by its source` (edge-mint authority check) + 1× `PayloadInvalid: Relation set owner must have a canonical world, identity, or instance address`; `pg-projection.integration.test.ts` — 2× projection restore/catch-up returning empty cells where one was expected. These may be pre-existing gaps rather than regressions from the current diff.

### Overall verdict

TypeScript is fully sound (both typecheck gates clean) and the overwhelming majority of the suite passes (6203 unit + 279 integration tests green). The in-flight kind-revision-binding arc has two concrete, reproducible regressions — a root-transaction guard rejecting legitimate nested-tx kind installs, and a legacy-lineage restore now throwing on previously-valid sealed snapshots — plus a Postgres-only Proxy/private-field crash in the same subsystem, and six more Postgres-only failures of unclear origin. Governance gates are red as expected mid-arc (structure-cycle/line-cap violations, a stale declarations pin, a PG_URL-gated proof gate, and one raw storage-address leak in a public test), consistent with the "P1.3(d) executing, gates RED" state already on record for this arc.</result>

Lock keys: every closure-member `binding:` key plus the `transition:` key via `canonicalKindBindingControlLockKeys` — the same keys apply passes as `currentSerializationGuards`, so a finalization transaction and an in-flight Domain apply mutually exclude; sorted canonical order prevents deadlock. Flow inside the transaction: (1) load rollout by id, reconstruct the closure exactly as rollout.ts prepareApplication does (same rollout-vs-reconstruction exact checks); (2) read every member control and classify: EXACT-FORMED (activeRolloutId === rolloutId, sequence === base+1, shape === formedControl) → proceed; ALL-FINALIZED (every affected control already shows activeRolloutId null, finalizedRevisionId === new side, sequence === formed+1, or lifecycle 'retired' for a null new side) → return `unchanged: true` (finalize-once replay, not an error); anything else → throw, transaction rolls back; (3) readiness (below); (4) the all-member CAS: for each affected binding build the finalized control — finalizedRevisionId = new revision, desiredRevisionId = same value (contract law: a stable active control desires its finalized revision), readableRevisionIds recomputed, activeRolloutId = null, lifecycle 'retired' with all-null revisions when the new side is null, sequence = nextKindBindingControlSequenceV1(formed sequence) — and issue `controls.compareAndSet({bindingId, sequence: formedSequence}, next)`; any result other than 'updated' throws and rolls back EVERY member and the row batch together; (5) the batch compatibility rewrite (below) in the same transaction. \"One all-member CAS\" therefore means N per-member CAS writes that commit or abort as one root transaction under all member locks — no state where some controls advanced. If any member's generation moved underneath (result 'stale'/'conflict'), the whole transaction aborts with a typed refusal naming the binding; the caller re-reads and either observes the ALL-FINALIZED replay (another finalizer won) or surfaces real drift. Mixed advancement is unreachable by construction, so observing it is corruption and refuses loudly. Composition: representation-neutral `composeKindBindingRolloutFinalizationV1` in a NEW packages/core/src/runtime/composition/kind-binding-finalization.ts (kind-binding-rollout.ts is 516/600 — do not grow it toward the ceiling); memory specialization there over the memory host; PG store in a NEW packages/server/src/adapters/kinds/revision-binding/finalization-pg.ts (control-pg.ts is already 762 under an exact exception — do not touch its ceiling). 4. `server/event-visibility.ts` — **flip** over `marker`, decisively. `resolveKindOverrideRow` probes uid→seed-uid→path and `resolveKindEventsForRef` reads the kind's authored `events` map to pick delivery audience. The `isKindRow` marker test is only the gate in front of a genuine catalog resolve.
5. `engine/invocation/port-resolution.ts` — **flip** over `marker`. Leg 2 (`kindSelfCandidates`) reads `dims['core:kind'].ports` to dispatch — dispatch-from-declaration, explicitly in the flip definition.
6. `engine/stream/session-contracts.ts` — **retire** over `marker`, weakly. It authors `core:kind` declaration content routed through `installKindDeclarationSetAtomicallyV1` (the legacy funnel), so it should stay on the ratchet; but the declaration text itself would survive a service-based install, so only its funnel is truly legacy.
7. `engine/deployment/kind-operations.ts` — **marker** over `retire`. Its only W1 is `PATCH_PROTECTED_KEYS['core:kind'] = '*'` — a table `assertPatchKeysAllowed` *throws* on. Counting a refuser as an author would inflate the retire population and flatter the real writers' shrink curve.
8. + 9. `adapters/memory/yi-repo.ts` and `pg-storage/yi/repository.ts` — **flip** over `universal`, for the same member on both representations: `kindRowsByOwnerPrefix` is a catalog enumeration (PG issues `WHERE origin_kind = 'core:kind' AND dimensions-&gt;'core:kind'-&gt;&gt;'id' LIKE …`). A repository that already carries a kind-literal facet cannot honestly be `universal`. Worth the owner's attention: extracting `kindRowsByOwnerPrefix` into its own member would let both repositories return to a clean `universal`.

## What I changed

**One correction to your setup.** The exact context grant you added (`views.streams`) was *sufficient* for `dependency-authorization`, but it then tripped a second law: `scripts/phase-1-structure/context-grants.mjs:269` requires every exact context root grant to be backed by an `architecture-change` record in the append-only architecture shadow (`packages/core/architecture-shadow/`). Appending one needs commit-bound evidence (`sourceArchiveCommitBindingError` binds archives to introduction commits), which I can't produce without committing — and it's a governance ceremony you didn't ask for.

So I removed the context edge instead, which I think is the better reading anyway: the only coupling was `viewOutputPositionOfSource`, and neither position space owns that join.

- `packages/core/src/views/contracts/position.ts` — dropped the `SourcePosition` import and moved `viewOutputPositionOfSource` out. Views now declares its own output order naming nothing external.
- `packages/core/src/runtime/composition/views.ts` — now hosts `viewOutputPositionOfSource`. This is the sole views composition root (`scripts/check-p-a-views.mjs:47`), and composing a View over a source is exactly where the two spaces meet.
- `packages/core/src/runtime/composition/public.ts` — re-exports it from `./views.js`.
- `packages/core/structure-map/destination/shells.json` — added `runtime/composition/views.ts` grants under `views.contract` and `streams.contract`.
- `packages/core/structure-map/destination/exact-context-root-grants.json` — **reverted to HEAD** (git no longer reports it modified), since no exact grant is needed.
- Tests moved with the function (`views/contracts/position.test.ts`, `runtime/composition/views.test.ts`).

**Consumers repointed to `@bonfire/core/composition`** (16 files; unrelated symbols kept on `/internal` via split import statements): server `ws-replay.ts`, `ws-subscription-shapes.ts`, `ws-positions.ts`, `ws-conn.ts`, `ws-subscriptions.test.ts`, `methods/projections/read.ts`, `methods/projections/explain.ts`, `ws/connection-replay.ts`, `ws/connection.ts`, `ws/connection-delivery.ts`, `ws/subscriptions/replay.ts`, `perf/pg-sdk-phase2-hot-paths.bench.ts`; sdk `client/typed-client.test.ts`, `client/rpc-schema/views.ts`.

Three files on your list needed no change — `ws/subscriptions/projection.ts`, `ws/subscriptions/delivery.ts`, and `sdk/client/subscription-position.ts(.test.ts)` only reference the *locally defined* `sourcePositionOfEvent` / `deliveryCursorAtSourcePosition`, not the core symbols. **Snapshots** regenerated via the repo's own mechanism (`UPDATE_CORE_PUBLIC_API_SNAPSHOT=1` / `UPDATE_CORE_PUBLIC_TYPES_SNAPSHOT=1 pnpm exec vitest run …`); all 12 position symbols now sit under `composition`. Shape:\n\n```ts\nimport type { SourcePosition } from '../../contracts/position.js'\n\nexport interface StreamEntryShape { readonly uid: string; readonly seq: number }\n\nexport interface DurableStreamRepository&lt;TEntry extends StreamEntryShape&gt; {\n  append(\n    entry: Omit&lt;TEntry, 'seq'&gt;,\n    opts?: { prehension?: ReadonlyArray&lt;string&gt; },\n  ): Promise&lt;{ entry: TEntry; eventUid: string }&gt;\n  read(page: { anchor: string; before?: SourcePosition; limit?: number }): Promise&lt;ReadonlyArray&lt;TEntry&gt;&gt;\n  replay(page: { anchor: string; after: SourcePosition; limit?: number }): Promise&lt;{\n    entries: ReadonlyArray&lt;TEntry&gt;\n    scannedTo: SourcePosition\n    head: SourcePosition\n  }&gt;\n}\n```\n\nMember justification against what the durable tier (stream table, 001_init.sql; MessagesStore in ports/stream.ts) actually does:\n- append: the only write. Takes the row minus `seq` (storage allocates the dense per-anchor position under the per-session advisory lock) plus the `prehension` opt. Returns the entry AND `eventUid` — load-bearing, not decoration: sendStream must chain each delta's event off the previous delta's event uid, which is unknowable outside the storage transaction (documented on MessagesStore.append). Dropping either return member makes the port unable to ever front message-write/message-stream, i.e. unable to be the pinned bridge. Caller loops `after = scannedTo` until `scannedTo &gt;= head`. Consumers: behavior proof now; View materializer build/rebuild in this same slice.\n\nDeliberately NOT in the port (each has zero consumers through this boundary; a port member with no consumer is debt): byUid, update (supersedable-entry evolution — moves when rewrite migrates), children/leaves (DAG-topology reads, the `dag` face), subscribe (the live lane; the work order admits watch only through the later versioned delta/cursor/reset contract), headSeq (subsumed by replay's `head` for boundary consumers), findStaleStreams (reaper/operations concern).
